# Agents · U · part 2

921 entries, ui-copy-reviewer to ui-integrity-reviewer. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **ui-copy-reviewer**: Reviews a diff for the wording of everything a user reads — button and menu labels that should be a verb but are a noun (or the reverse), generic OK/Yes/Submit on a decision, unnatural word order, inconsistent terminology for one action, list and timeline rows worded as commands, error and empty-state text that does not say what to do next. Use when reviewing a diff that adds or changes user-facing strings. ([galilei2050/belay](https://github.com/galilei2050/belay/blob/HEAD/plugins/usable-ui/agents/ui-copy-reviewer.md) · ★2)
